### 2026-10-11 — Every action says when it's done (US-OPS-38, US-CAT-16, D-223)

- **One toast host (US-OPS-38):** `apps/web/src/components/toast-host.tsx` — `ToastHost` in the app layout and `useToast()` anywhere under it. The layout stays mounted across pages, so a toast shown just before `router.push` shows on the page that opens. Toasts stack newest on top, 3 at most by default (2 at phone width), a new one over the maximum closing the oldest at once; each closes after 5 seconds by default, paused while the stack is hovered or a toast focused. The maximum and the interval are props on the host; a toast can set its own interval. Replaces the ten `entity-toast.tsx` copies (each page had its own provider and viewport, so a toast died with its page and a second one replaced the first), the contacts `sessionStorage` hand-off and the payments `?deleted=` and new job's `?created=` hand-offs. The permission toast (US-OPS-12) shows through the same host.
- **The toasts that were missing:** deleting a lead and marking it lost (with its reason, or where to add one); deleting a meeting or one occurrence; deleting a quote or an invoice from its page; revising, converting to an invoice or a job and duplicating a quote (from its page or a row); issuing a credit note from the list; recording a payment from the list ("RCP-0032 recorded" with the credit left or what it paid); completing and invoicing a milestone, applying a variation and removing someone from a job's crew; deleting a refund; removing an attachment.
- **`@spine/ui` Toast:** the viewport lays toasts out newest on top (`flex-col-reverse`) and spans the bottom of the screen below `sm`; a toast slides in from the right (not with Reduce Motion), its close sits at the top, and in dark mode it has a thin outline. The Toast doc page shows a stack.
- **Errors stay where the action was:** a toast no longer carries an error in the refined modules. Deleting a payment, Retry now and Cancel on the email log, the invoices list's row and bulk actions, the quotes list's revise, duplicate and customer link, the catalogue's bulk changes and pausing or resuming a recurring invoice or bill now show what went wrong above the list or page (`components/action-error.tsx`, with a close); a failed bulk change keeps the selection. Banking and expenses keep theirs until they're refined.
- **Bulk changes ask first (US-CAT-16):** the catalogue's bulk Set tax asks "Change the tax on 12 items?" with the new tax and that issued quotes and invoices keep theirs; Set category asks "Move 12 items to “Labour”?"; Set income account asks "Change the income account on 12 items?". Go back keeps the selection. Archive and Restore still don't ask.
- **Docs:** D-223, `09-design-system.md`, the Toast component doc and CLAUDE.md carry the rule; screens `ToastAfterNavigate`, `ToastStack`, `ToastStackDark`, `ToastStackPhone`, `CatalogBulkConfirm`.
- **Tests:** web unit `us-ops-38` (the maximum, phone width, the interval and ×, no host, the toast copy, the error message); UI a11y `toast` (a stack of three); E2E `us-ops-38` (record from the list, lead deleted and lost, the stack, deletes from a record's page, a failed delete shown on the page) and `us-cat-16`, with `us-cat-12` confirming its Set tax. QA script section 215.

### 2026-10-11 — Menus that read at a glance (US-OPS-37, D-222)

- **One menu style:** `DropdownMenuItem`, `DropdownMenuSubTrigger` and the `ContextMenu` items now set their icon's size (14 px), colour (muted; red on a destructive item) and spacing (10 px before the label), with rows at least 32 px tall; menus are at least 12rem wide. Before, each screen spaced its own icons — a typed space on the quotes menu, `gap-2` on the lead card, nothing on the email log and the payment page — and most refined menus had none.
- **One icon per action (D-222):** `apps/web/src/lib/menu-icons.ts`, used by every action menu in the refined modules: Contacts and Catalogue (rows, bundles), the Pipeline lead card, the meeting quick view, Quotes (row, page's More, line), Jobs (page's More), Invoices (row, page's More, Payments), Credit notes (row, page's More), Payments (row, page's More, Applied to) and the Email log. Destructive items come last after a divider (the bundles menu's Delete is now marked destructive; the email log's Cancel is red and last). Choice menus keep their dots and ticks. Other modules get the spacing now and their icons when refined.
- **Docs:** the DropdownMenu and ContextMenu doc pages show icons, a destructive item and a divider; the component docs, `09-design-system.md` and CLAUDE.md carry the rule.
- **Tests:** UI a11y `dropdown-menu` (icons hidden from assistive tech, the classes), web unit `us-ops-37`, E2E `us-ops-37` (measures the gap, row height, icon size and colours on the invoice menus). QA script section 214.

### 2026-10-11 — Payments you can work from (US-PAY-19 to US-PAY-26, D-221)

- **A payment page (US-PAY-19, US-PAY-20):** `/payments/:id` replaces the drawer — its state (Fully applied, Credit left, Refunded), one next step (Apply credit, else Email receipt, else Download PDF), a strip of Received, Applied, Refunded and Credit left, "Applied to" with what each invoice still owes, notes, refunds, attachments and Activity (`GET /payments/:id/activity`: recorded, applied, moved off, receipt emailed, refunded). This fixes links that went nowhere: the "Payment received" notification, ledger drill-downs, the bank register, invoices' Payments and Activity and the email log now open the page; `/payments?id=` redirects to it.
- **The list (US-PAY-21, US-PAY-22):** cards for Received this month, Credit to use (a filter) and Refunded this month; rows show the reference, the account, what each payment was applied to and the credit left; newest first (it was oldest first); contact, method and date range filters, search, card, sort and page in the address, with Refresh; a row menu that opens the page's dialogs. `GET /payments` rows gain `appliedTo`, `refundedAmount` and `moneyAccountName`.
- **One Record payment dialog (US-PAY-23, US-PAY-24):** `components/record-payment-dialog.tsx` replaces the payments page's and the invoice page's copies (and the bank statement line's). The customer's open invoices are a checklist oldest due first with how late each is; ticking fills the amount, a typed amount is spread oldest first, and what goes on the invoices and what becomes credit is said before the button. "Email a receipt to …" is a switch (`POST /payments` `sendReceipt`). A payment from the same customer for the same amount within 3 days, or with the same reference, is flagged with a link to it (`GET /payments/possible-duplicates`).
- **Search (US-PAY-25):** a Payments group, by receipt number, reference or customer.
- **Move off an invoice (US-PAY-26):** from the payment's "Applied to" or the invoice's Payments, "Move off …" asks first with an optional reason, removes the allocation, posts P30 (Dr 1100 AR / Cr 2200 Customer Advances, dated today) and audits it on both; the money becomes the payment's credit. Not for deposit or void invoices. `POST /payments/:id/allocations/:allocationId/unapply`.
- **Demo:** demo payments no longer email a receipt to the demo customer, and a payment dated on its own day has its "Recorded" audit entry moved there too, so its page's Activity reads in order with names (checked for every demo in `us-onb-24-28`). The mobile app doesn't use any of these routes; its typecheck and tests pass unchanged.
- **Tests:** API integration `us-pay-19-26`; web unit `us-pay-20` to `us-pay-23`; E2E `us-pay-19-26`, with US-PAY-17, US-PAY-18, US-BANK-02, US-BANK-03, US-BANK-10, US-INV-32 and the quote-to-payment flow moved to the page and the new dialog. QA script section 213.

### 2026-10-11 — The email log (US-SET-27 to US-SET-29, US-DASH-30, D-220)

- **Every email, recorded (US-SET-27):** a new `email_log` table (migration 0114) holds every email Spine sends — when, to whom, the subject, the kind, the record it's about, the status, attempts and the last error; no bodies. Queued emails (quote, invoice, reminder, notification) go through `EmailLogService.enqueue`, and their workers wrap each try in `attempt`, which moves the row through Sending to Sent, Retrying or Failed. Emails sent straight away or by their own job (credit note, receipt, refund, statement, invitation, password reset, test, daily digest, data export) pass `log` to `EmailService.send`. A nightly `email-log-cleanup` job removes rows older than 180 days.
- **The page (US-SET-27):** `/emails`, Owner-only, from Settings › Emails ("Email log") and from a quote's or invoice's "Email failed" message ("See it in the email log", filtered to the record). Cards for Waiting, Failed in the last 7 days (filters) and Sent today; status, kind, range and search; the list in the address with Refresh (D-210).
- **Retry and cancel (US-SET-28):** "Retry now" on a failed or retrying queued email re-queues it with its stored job (attempts start over); "Cancel" stops a queued or retrying one before it sends, and the quote or invoice says "Email not sent — cancelled". Each asks first (D-218) and is audited. `POST /emails/:id/retry`, `POST /emails/:id/cancel`.
- **Not set up (US-SET-29):** with no SMTP server, the log and Settings › Emails say so and how many emails are waiting; a queued email isn't tried meanwhile (no attempts used) and goes out when Spine starts with SMTP set. When the last 3 queued emails in a row failed, the warning gives the error. `GET /emails/status`.
- **Dashboard (US-DASH-30):** an Owner's Needs your attention lists "<n> emails failed", naming the first; Review opens the log filtered to Failed. The digest's table names it "Emails that failed".
- **Retired (US-QUO-07):** the quotes list's "Email queue" dialog, `GET /quotes/email-queue` and `QueueService.listPendingJobs`.
- **Tests:** API integration `us-set-27-29` (with US-DASH-30); web unit `us-set-27`, `us-set-28`, `us-set-29`, `us-dash-30`; E2E `us-set-27-28`. Tests that build services by hand pass the email log. QA script section 212.

### 2026-10-11 — The meeting quick view opens in place (US-MTG-14 fix)

A late meeting's quick view opened without its attendees, which load a moment later; when they arrived it grew past the bottom of the window for a frame, then jumped up to fit. It now shows the Attendees heading with a placeholder row while they load, so it opens at its full height and is placed once. This is what made "a late meeting's quick view stays inside the window" fail about half the time. The test now records the quick view's edges on every frame from the click, so a jump past the window fails it every time. QA script section 204 notes the check.

### 2026-10-11 — Credit notes you can work from (US-INV-36 to US-INV-41, US-ONB-29, D-219)

- **Credit with the tax (US-INV-36):** the issue dialog credits the invoice's own lines — ticked lines and how many, or an amount spread over the lines by what's left on each (`spreadCreditAmount`) — and works each credit line out from its invoice line's figures in proportion, so tax (by component), discounts and tax-inclusive prices come back exactly as invoiced. Before, the dialog made one untaxed free-text line, so a credit note never reduced the tax owed. The totals, tax by name and the split show before the button (`POST /credit-notes/preview`); a line or invoice can't be credited past what's left (`GET /credit-notes/creditable`). `credit_note_lines` gains `invoice_line_id`, `tax_rate_id` and `tax_group_id` (migration 0113).
- **New credit note (US-INV-37):** from the list, pick the invoice (`leftToCredit` on `GET /invoices`), then the same dialog; the new credit note's page opens.
- **A credit note page (US-INV-38, US-INV-39):** `/credit-notes/:id` replaces the drawer — one next step (Apply credit, Email, Download PDF), a strip of Credited, Off the invoice, Applied, Refunded and Credit left, the lines with tax by name, refunds, attachments and Activity (`GET /credit-notes/:id/activity`). Apply credit spreads the credit over the customer's open invoices. Invoices, their Activity, ledger drill-downs and search (a new Credit notes group) link to it.
- **The list (US-INV-40, US-INV-41):** cards for Credited this month, Credit to use (a filter) and Refunded this month; rows show the reason and where each credit went, with a row menu; search, filters, card, sort and page in the address, with Refresh. `use-credit-note-stats.ts` and `credit-note-detail-drawer.tsx` are gone.
- **Invoice page:** the Paid bar counts payments only; "Issue credit note" shows only while something's left to credit.
- **Fix:** the invoice page's "Issue anyway" (the HSN/SAC question) had its handler written as `() => void …`, which US-OPS-10's check for red delete/void buttons read as "void" and failed on; it passed earlier only because Turborepo served the API tests from cache (they read the web app's files, which aren't in the API's cache inputs). The handler is now named.
- **Demo (US-ONB-29):** each demo has three credit notes crediting lines with tax — off the invoice, applied (industry demos) or kept to use, and refunded — dated on their own days this month. A refund of a demo credit note's credit is flagged demo and deleted with the demo, with any applied credit.
- **Tests:** API integration `us-inv-36-41`, demo `us-onb-24-28` (US-ONB-29 added), US-PAY-16's helper and US-ONB-17's numbers; shared `us-onb-24-28` (all demos); web unit `us-inv-36`, `us-inv-39` to `us-inv-41`; E2E `us-inv-36` to `us-inv-41`, `us-onb-29`, with US-PAY-16 and the route smoke test updated. QA script section 211.

### 2026-10-11 — A major action asks first (D-218)

Anything that can't be taken back, changes the books, emails a customer or acts on many records asks before it happens, with `useConfirm()` — a new hook in `@spine/ui` (AlertDialog doc page and a11y test). Newly confirmed: Record and Delete draft on vendor bills (page and row menu); Record, Remove from bill and Delete refund on vendor credits; bulk Approve on expenses; Revise (page and row menu), Convert to invoice, Convert to job and Approve discount on quotes; removing someone from a job's crew and applying a variation to the contract; removing opening invoices, bills and credits; deleting tax payments and customer refunds; finishing a bank reconciliation; cancelling an invitation. Each question says what happens to the data; delete, void and remove are red (D-058). Invoices' own confirmations are in the entry below (D-217). CLAUDE.md and the design-system rules carry the rule. Tests: `useConfirm` a11y and behaviour (`@spine/ui`); E2E `d-218` (Go back changes nothing, the verb acts); the quote, banking, purchases, payments and tax specs answer the new questions. QA script section 210.

### 2026-10-11 — Invoices you can work from (US-INV-27 to US-INV-35, US-ONB-28, D-217)

The invoices list, the invoice page and the builder are brought up to the refined quotes and jobs screens.

- **Cards (US-INV-27):** Outstanding, Overdue and Due in 7 days show balance totals with counts, in the business's currency ("+ other currencies" when some aren't), and filter the list; Paid this month adds up the payments dated this month that paid invoices. `use-invoice-stats.ts` is gone; the cards read the list. "Received since 1 Oct" is the first of the month in the business's locale, shown once payments load (it had been built from the browser's locale on the server too, so a day-first browser hit a hydration error).
- **The list (US-INV-28, US-INV-29):** search, statuses, contact, card, sort and page live in the address (D-210), with Refresh and a quiet refresh after 30 s away; search covers numbers, customers, line names and jobs. Each row has one status (Draft, Due, Part paid, Paid, Overdue, Void), what kind of invoice it is under the number ("Progress claim 2 · JOB-002", "Deposit"), the issue date, a due countdown or "Paid 2 Oct", and the balance with a bar of what's paid; the row menu offers Issue, Record payment, Email invoice, Send reminder, Download PDF, Duplicate and Void, opening the invoice with `?action=`. `GET /invoices` rows gain `lineNames`, `job` and `lastPaidAt`.
- **The invoice page (US-INV-30 to US-INV-32):** the number is the page title, with its status, a claim badge, the customer, Issued and Due (with the countdown), the place of supply and the job, milestone or quote it came from. One primary button is the next step (Issue invoice, Send reminder, Record payment, else Download PDF), the rest beside it or under More; a strip reads Total, Paid (last date and bar), Retention held or Credited, and Balance due. The lines are a table with the tax by name, Retention held, Paid and Balance due under them (`DocumentLinesTable`, shared with quotes; `GET /invoices/:id` gains `sourceQuoteNumber`, `lastPaidAt` and `taxLines`); payments, credit notes and attachments follow. An Activity panel (`GET /invoices/:id/activity`) tells the invoice's story — created, from a quote or milestone, duplicated, edited, issued, emailed, reminded, paid (with method and receipt link), credited, voided — newest first. Deleting a draft now asks first.
- **Payment terms (US-INV-33):** Settings › Documents › Invoices has "Invoices due in" (0–365 days, 30 to start). Every issued invoice gets a due date — issue date plus the terms — when it has none, retention releases included, so it can go overdue and be reminded. The builder starts at today plus the terms with On receipt, +15, +30 and +60 quick picks.
- **The builder (US-INV-34):** the quote builder's compact line rows, a bottom bar that stays in view (Subtotal, Tax, Total, Cancel, Preview PDF, Save draft, Save and issue) and "Leave without saving?" — the bar and the guard are now shared (`DocumentBuilderBar`, `useUnsavedGuard`). Preview PDF (`POST /invoices/preview-pdf`) renders the invoice on screen with a "Preview only — not a valid invoice" watermark and saves nothing; a draft's PDF carries the watermark too.
- **Confirm before acting (D-217):** Issue invoice asks "Issue this invoice?" (who will owe what, when it's due, and that it can then only be voided or credited) unless the HSN/SAC or credit question already asks; Delete draft asks; the bulk "Send reminders" asks "Send N payment reminders?". The row menu's Send reminder and the dashboard's one-overdue-invoice "Send reminder" (US-DASH-13) now open the invoice with the reminder email to check and send, instead of emailing the customer in one click.
- **Duplicate (US-INV-35):** `POST /invoices/:id/duplicate` makes a new draft with the same customer, currency, lines, notes and terms (a deposit stays a deposit; other types become standard); the builder opens it dated today, due on the terms, with "Duplicated from INV-…".
- **Demo (US-ONB-28):** every demo has an invoice due this week and one overdue (Riverstone's deposit is due in 5 days); some invoices were emailed on their issue day and reminded once late, recorded as the app records them (no real email is queued); invoices, milestone invoices and payments carry their own days in Activity.
- **StatCard:** a `critical` tone (red value), with its docs page and a11y test.
- **Tests:** API integration `us-inv-27-35`, `us-onb-24-28`; web unit `us-inv-27` to `us-inv-29`, `us-inv-32`, `us-inv-33`; shared `us-onb-24-28` (all demos); E2E `us-inv-27` to `us-inv-35`, `us-onb-28`, with the invoice and quote specs updated for the moved actions; US-OPS-21 opens the list filtered to void invoices, US-PAY-10 waits for the receivables rather than the loading rows, US-QUO-31 matches the toast's text exactly, and US-OPS-22 searches for an overdue invoice it looks up rather than "INV-0002", which the job specs now create (all four were order-dependent on the shared demo org). QA script section 209.

### 2026-10-11 — Demo data that shows the refined screens (US-ONB-24 to US-ONB-27, D-216)

Every demo — Riverstone Builders and each industry × country — is brought up to the refinements, still through the real services, with past dates written the way the app would have written them (audit trail included). The dev seed (`pnpm db:seed:dev`) is unchanged: it still creates only the owner, the organisation and its accounts.

- **Pipeline (US-ONB-24):** the demo Staff and Field people are created first, so open leads belong to the owner and Sarah Mendes (Staff); each has spent its own time in its stage, has an expected close date and a dated stage history (created, then each stage a week apart); one has gone cold ("No activity for 19 days"); won leads closed at different ages; each demo has one lead lost on price, with a note (industry demos: a tender lost by the regional customer).
- **Quotes (US-ONB-25):** every quote has a title; sent quotes went out on different days and the customer has viewed some (`quote_events`); one expires this week and one has expired; one was accepted this month and one declined with a comment, through the customer's link; quotes link to their leads; the newest job was made from an accepted contract quote (one lump-sum line at its contract value), so it says "From QUO-…" and the quote says "Converted to a job".
- **Jobs (US-ONB-26):** every job has an end date and a cost budget, the newest near it; the job in progress has a completed milestone waiting to be invoiced and a % complete estimate; the finished job is Completed; milestones, photos and the job's history carry their own dates; customers with a job have a site address.
- **Catalogue and calendar (US-ONB-27):** goods post to 4000 Sales — Goods; each demo has two bundles (recorded with the demo and deleted with it); meetings start at different times, with three or more in the next two weeks.
- **Fixes:** a tax group's rates now always read in the same order (plain before compound, then by name — "CGST (9%) + SGST (9%)"), where it used to depend on the database; `POST /quotes/preview-pdf` is listed with the routes that take no Idempotency-Key (it writes nothing).
- **Tests:** `us-onb-24-27` shared (all 35 demos, Riverstone included) and API integration (Riverstone, India construction, UK IT services); US-ONB-18's quote-states check includes accepted and declined; the Job Profitability, US-CRM-19 and US-JOB-31 E2E follow the new demo (the finished job is completed; leads have owners; the demo has a milestone ready to bill). QA script section 208.

### 2026-10-11 — Jobs you can run from one place (US-JOB-30 to US-JOB-38, D-215)

- **New job (US-JOB-30):** "New job" in the Jobs toolbar and empty state opens a drawer: Contact, Job name, Site address (filled from the contact's site address, else its billing address), Currency, Contract value, Retention %, Start and End dates and Notes. "Start from an accepted quote" converts one of the accepted quotes that has no job yet, with the repeat-conversion warning. The new job opens on Milestones ("Set up how this job is billed", "Add milestones") with "Job JOB-007 created". `POST /jobs` refuses an end date before the start ("End the job on or after its start date.").
- **The list (US-JOB-31 to US-JOB-33):** cards Active (with contract value), Ready to bill (completed, not invoiced milestones, with the amount), Over or near budget (job-profitability right only) and Retention held (from each job's 1150 lines, "Across N jobs"); the first three filter. Search (number, name, contact, site), status, contact (only contacts with a job), card, sort and page live in the address, with Refresh and a quiet refresh after 30 s away. Rows show the number and site under the name, the next milestone with "Due in 3 days" or "Overdue 2 days" (or "All billed"), complete % beside billed % (over the contract flagged, "Billed over the contract"), the end date and the contract value; Budget used and Retention moved into Columns. `GET /jobs` and `GET /jobs/:id` take `include=billing` (`billedAmount`, `billedPct`, `readyToBillCount`, `readyToBillAmount`, `retentionHeld`; Owners and Staff).
- **The job page (US-JOB-34, US-JOB-35):** the number is the page title with a status menu (saves at once; Cancelled asks first), the name, contact (a link), site (opens a map), dates and "From QUO-0031" (`GET /jobs/:id` gains `sourceQuoteNumber`). One primary button: Invoice <milestone>, Complete <milestone> or Release retention; Edit job, Raise variation and More beside it. A strip of figures: Contract (with applied variations), Billed, Cost against budget, Margin and % complete — Field users see only % complete. Tabs show counts. Milestones read as progress: "Billed X of Y" with a bar, a step marker per milestone, its due state (overdue in the overdue colour) and its action; a completed job shows the retention held.
- **Photos (US-JOB-36):** "Add photos" (or a drop on the card) uploads several at once, each on its own tile; one that fails says why and the rest carry on. Photos are grouped under Site photos and each milestone; one opens full size with its caption, who added it and when, Previous and Next (← →), Download and Close (Esc). `GET /jobs/:id/photos` rows gain `uploadedByName`.
- **Activity (US-JOB-37):** `GET /jobs/:id/activity` (Owners and Staff) and a panel beside the milestones: created (from its quote), status changes, milestones set, completed and invoiced, variations raised and applied, photos (added together, one entry), notes, crew and retention released, with who and when; "Show N earlier" past eight.
- **Demo photos (US-JOB-38):** demo jobs get six real site photos from Unsplash (Unsplash License, bundled in `apps/api/assets/demo` with `SOURCES.md`, each under 150 KB) in place of the 1 × 1 placeholder.
- **Tests:** `us-job-30` to `us-job-37` web unit; `us-job-30` to `us-job-38` E2E; `us-job-30-37` and `us-job-38` API integration. US-JOB-22, US-JOB-25, US-JOB-27 and US-SET-25's E2E follow the new header and list. QA script section 207.
- **Design:** canvas "10 Jobs"; seven artboards.

### 2026-10-10 — Quotes you can work from (US-QUO-27 to US-QUO-36, D-214)

- **Cards (US-QUO-27):** Open (with its value), Expiring in 7 days, Won this month (with its value) and Win rate over 90 days, counting each quote once as its newest version (the old "Total quotes" counted versions, so it didn't match the list). The first three filter, pressed state in the address.
- **The list (US-QUO-28, US-QUO-29):** search (number, title, contact and line names), status, contact (only contacts with a quote), card, sort and page live in the address; Refresh and "Updated … ago"; newest first. Rows show the title under the number, "Draft" in italics, "Viewed 2×" with the last view, Sent, and a validity countdown ("in 5 days", "Expired 6 days ago"); dates follow the business's locale. The row menu offers View, Edit, Send, Copy customer link, Download PDF, Duplicate, Convert, Revise and Delete as allowed. `GET /quotes` rows gain `viewCount`, `lastViewedAt` and `lineNames`.
- **A title (US-QUO-30):** `quotes.title` (migration `0112`), up to 120 characters, copied by duplicate and revise, started from a lead's title, and shown on the quote page, the PDF and the customer's page.
- **The quote page (US-QUO-31 to US-QUO-33):** the number is the page title, with the status, title, contact link, sent date and validity countdown; one primary button for the next step (Send quote, Copy customer link, Convert to invoice, Revise). Copy customer link mints a fresh link without emailing (`POST /quotes/:id/link`). Lines are a table, with GST tax by name in the totals and Discount only when there is one. An Activity panel (`GET /quotes/:id/activity`) and a Related panel (`GET /quotes/:id/related`: lead, versions, jobs, invoices); `/leads?lead=<id>` opens a lead in the drawer.
- **The builder (US-QUO-34 to US-QUO-36):** one row per item with its description, HSN/SAC and supply type under it; drag by the handle, the row menu or Alt + ↑/↓, with the move announced; Enter in the last row's tax adds a row. A bar at the bottom keeps the totals, Cancel, Preview PDF (the quote as it is on screen, without saving: `POST /quotes/preview-pdf`; every unsent quote's PDF is watermarked "DRAFT — Preview only — not a valid quote"), Save draft and Save and send in view; leaving with unsaved changes (Cancel, a link, Back, closing the tab) asks first. Valid until starts from Settings › Documents › "Quotes valid for" (`document_layout.quote.validDays`, 30), with +15, +30 and +60 day picks; a duplicate of a quote with no validity uses it too.
- **Fixes:** a rich-text editor's placeholder sat at the bottom of the page when the box was empty (every screen); the quote builder's Contact picker had no accessible name; on quote, invoice and credit-note PDFs a text line printed bold like a heading — it's now plain, smaller and lighter (and on the customer's quote page a heading stands out from text).
- **Components:** `StatCard` takes `detail` (a figure beside the value) and `tone` ("warning").
- **Tests:** `us-quo-27`, `28`, `29`, `32` and `36` web unit; `us-quo-27` to `us-quo-36` E2E; `us-quo-29-36` API integration; StatCard and RichTextEditor tests. QA script section 206.
- **Design:** canvas "09 Quotes"; six artboards.

### 2026-10-10 — A catalogue that's easy to keep (US-CAT-10 to US-CAT-15, D-213)

- **Setup cards (US-CAT-10):** "Missing tax rate" and "No income account" ("Posts to 4100 Sales — Services") are filters you press, with their pressed state in the address; "Total items" clears; "Categories" names the first two. Missing tax counts taxable items with no tax; for a GST business zero-rated items don't count, elsewhere any untaxed item that isn't exempt or non-taxable does.
- **The table (US-CAT-11):** the Tax column names the tax ("GST 18%", "Exempt", "Zero-rated"), and "No tax" on a taxable item has a warning icon in the due-soon colour; the description sits under the name; HSN/SAC shows for a GST business; only archived rows carry a badge (the Status column is gone).
- **Many at once (US-CAT-12):** tick rows (or the header box for the page) for a bar with Set category, Set tax, Set income account, Archive or Restore, Make a bundle and Clear. One save through `POST /catalog-items/bulk` changes every item or none, checking each item's own/others rights first. For a GST business a GST group brings the matching IGST rate as the inter-state tax. Duplicate opens Add item filled from the row. Export downloads `catalogue-<date>.csv` with the list as filtered, in Import's columns plus Category, which Import now reads.
- **Coming back (US-CAT-13):** search, status, category, price, setup card, sort and page live in the address; one Price button holds Min and Max; Refresh with "Updated … ago", and a quiet refresh after 30 s away, as on Contacts.
- **Bundles (US-CAT-14):** the tabs show counts; Make a bundle takes a name, an optional description and a quantity per item and saves it at each item's default price and tax (`POST /catalog-bundles` with `items`); items in two currencies can't share one. Bundles has an empty state with "Choose items".
- **The drawer (US-CAT-15):** fields grouped under Details, Price and tax, and Accounts; "Used on 4 quotes and 2 invoices · last quoted 3 Oct 2026" (`GET /catalog-items/:id/usage`, Owner and Staff); deleting a used item says how many documents keep their own copy of the line.
- **Components:** `Table` takes `selected` / `onSelectedChange` / `selectLabel` (a checkbox column) and a controlled `sort` / `onSortChange`; `DataTable` passes them through and takes `defaultHidden`; `Checkbox` draws its indeterminate state.
- **Fix (D-210 lists):** Pipeline, Contacts, Catalogue and Meetings now write their state to the address with the browser's `replaceState` (`lib/replace-query.ts`) instead of `router.replace`. In a production build each `router.replace` went to the server, so an earlier keystroke could land after a later one and leave a stale search in the address (US-CRM-28's Back check failed against the production build); the spec's address check is now exact.
- **Tests:** `us-cat-10` to `us-cat-15` web unit and E2E; `us-cat-12`, `us-cat-14` and `us-cat-15` API integration tests; Table selection and sort tests. QA script section 205.
- **Design:** canvas "08 Catalogue"; six artboards.

### 2026-10-10 — A calendar that's easy to use (US-MTG-13 to US-MTG-17, D-212)

- **Mini calendar (US-MTG-13):** one dot under each day with a meeting you can see (recurring meetings on each day they occur, follow-ups too when My Activities is ticked), with the count on hover and for screen readers; the week or day on screen is shaded as one band; it follows the main view's month and loads the dots for any month it's paged to.
- **New meeting and the working day (US-MTG-14):** a New meeting button (30 minutes from the next half hour, or 9:00 AM on another day; hidden without the right to create meetings). Week and Day open at 8 AM, or at the hour before now on today, with the day headers kept in view and a now line on today. The quick view flips, shifts and scrolls so it always fits in the window.
- **Sidebar lists (US-MTG-15):** Upcoming lists the next 8 meetings in the next 60 days with their day and time and opens them on the calendar; Meetings by contact counts the range in the heading and links to each contact.
- **Coming back (US-MTG-16):** the view and date live in the address (`/meetings?view=month&date=2026-10-12`) and the last view is remembered on the device; dashboard and notification links still work and a notification link keeps the view and date.
- **Every screen (US-MTG-17):** the calendar fills the window (the pipeline shares the same `useFillHeight` hook, which now uses the page's own bottom padding). Under 768 px it opens in Agenda with no Week, the toolbar wraps and Agenda rows stack; under 1024 px a toolbar button opens the sidebar in a sheet. Agenda rows show the time range, place and contact (or "Internal").
- **Components:** `MiniCalendar` takes `marks`, `markLabel`, `highlight` and a controlled `month` / `onMonthChange` (`miniCalendarDayKey` exported). `Calendar` takes `toolbarAction`, `views`, `createRequest`, `openRequest` and `agendaDetails`, scrolls to the working day (`calendarScrollHour`), draws the now line and exports `expandEventsInRange`.
- **Tests:** `us-mtg-13` to `us-mtg-17` web unit and E2E; MiniCalendar and Calendar tests for marks, the band, the controlled month, views, the create request, the now line and agenda details. QA script section 204.
- **Known gap:** the app's left navigation has no phone layout yet; on a phone it has to be collapsed to use the calendar.
- **Design:** canvas "07 Meetings"; six artboards.

### 2026-10-10 — A pipeline that works (US-CRM-24 to US-CRM-28, D-211)

- **The board (US-CRM-24):**
  - Open stages share the width (six fit at 1280 px) and every column, Won and Lost included, runs the full height of the page; a long column scrolls on its own. When the stages don't fit, a shadow at the edge says there's more.
  - Won and Lost are slim columns with the last 30 days' count and total; pressing one opens it (newest first) and "Hide" closes it. Older closed leads show by choosing that stage in the stage filter.
  - A summary strip: open pipeline (per currency), won this month, win rate (won ÷ won and lost, closed in the last 90 days) and overdue follow-ups, which filters the board. An empty open column says "No leads in this stage".
- **Cards (US-CRM-25):** owner initials (name on hover; "—" when unassigned), "12 days in stage", the next follow-up ("Call · Tomorrow 10:00", in the overdue colour when past due), "No activity for N days" after 14 quiet days, the lost reason on lost cards and "Won 2 Oct" on won ones. A card is one button (Tab, Enter); its "⋯" menu (or the menu key) has Open, Move to… and Delete, and a move is announced ("Moved to Proposal").
- **Won and lost (US-CRM-26):** moving to Lost (drag, menu or drawer) asks why: Price, Timing, Went with a competitor, No response, Other, and a note; "Skip" moves it without one, and leaving Lost clears it. Moving to Won offers "Create quote" to people who can create quotes: the new quote takes the lead's contact and currency, says which lead it's for, and is linked to it (`quotes.lead_id`, kept on revisions).
- **The lead drawer (US-CRM-27):** replaces the lead dialog on the board, with owner (any active member or Unassigned) and expected close date, the lead's follow-ups (log an activity, add a follow-up, tick one done), its quotes and its history (created and each stage change, from the audit log). "Add lead" opens it at the column's stage.
- **Filters and refresh (US-CRM-28):** owner ("My leads", each member, "Unassigned") and source filters join search, stage, sort and View; they all live in the address (`/leads?owner=me&source=Referral`). Refresh and "Updated … ago" work as on Contacts, including a refresh after 30 seconds away.
- **API and data:** migration `0111` adds `leads.stage_changed_at` (backfilled from `updated_at`), `expected_close_date`, `lost_reason`, `lost_note` and `quotes.lead_id`. `GET /leads` adds `stageChangedAt`, `lastActivityAt` and `nextFollowUp`; `PUT /leads/:id` takes `ownerUserId` (an active member), `expectedCloseDate`, `lostReason` and `lostNote`; `GET /leads/:id/history`; `POST /quotes` takes `leadId` (same contact) and `GET /quotes?leadId=` lists a lead's quotes. Removing a stage that still has leads also restarts their days in stage.
- **Components:** `KanbanColumn` takes `collapsed` / `onExpand` / `expandLabel` (a slim column), `accent` and `headerAction`; `KanbanCard` takes `onOpen` / `label` (the card is one focusable button), `menu` and `onMenuKey`; `KanbanBoard` shows edge shadows. Cards no longer widen a column with long text. `ActivitiesSection` takes `leadId`, `heading` and `followUps`.
- **Tests:**
  - New: `us-crm-25`, `us-crm-26` and `us-crm-27` API integration; `us-crm-24`, `us-crm-25` and `us-crm-28` web unit; `us-crm-24` to `us-crm-28` E2E; Kanban a11y and keyboard tests.
  - `us-crm-19` and `us-crm-20` read the board's stage names from the columns, since Won and Lost no longer have headings.
  - Native drag and drop still can't be driven in Playwright, so the E2E tests move cards with the menu, which runs the same code as a drop. Cold-lead nudges need 14 days to pass, so the unit tests cover them.
  - QA script section 203.
- **Design:** canvas "06 Pipeline"; six artboards.

### 2026-10-10 — Contacts list polish and one look for toggles (US-OPS-36, US-CRM-21 to US-CRM-23, D-210)

- **Toggles (US-OPS-36):**
  - Every toggle group is one segmented control: a grey track and the chosen option as a raised chip with a primary outline and a semibold, primary label (new token `control-selected`). The contacts view switcher, Accrual / Cash (dashboard and reports), 3M / 6M / 12M, the theme switch (Profile and the user menu) and the expense entry mode drop their own styling. A single `Toggle` that's on gets a primary tint and outline.
  - The chosen style reads `aria-checked` / `aria-pressed`, not `data-state`, because a tooltip trigger sets its own `data-state`.
  - Icon-only options show their name as a tooltip on hover and keyboard focus, using the `@spine/ui` `Tooltip` (`tooltip` prop on `Toggle` and `ToggleGroupItem`; defaults to the `aria-label`). `TooltipProvider` is now mounted in the web and design-system root layouts.
- **Long names and emails (US-CRM-21):** in cards, names wrap to two lines and end with "…", clear of the "⋯" menu; emails and phones stay on one line; the full text shows on hover. In the table, names and emails are cut short so every column, Status included, fits at 1280 px.
- **Refresh (US-CRM-22):** a Refresh button (tooltip "Refresh list") with "Updated … ago" reloads contacts, the stat cards and the lead count in place; the list also refreshes when the tab is shown again after 30 seconds away. A failed refresh keeps the list and offers "Try again"; screen readers hear "Contacts updated".
- **Search, filters and stat cards (US-CRM-23):**
  - Search has its own row in every view (with Columns on the right in the Table view).
  - Type, status, search and page live in the address, so Back and bookmarks return to the same list.
  - The page loads every contact once and filters in the browser, so the stat cards count all active contacts. "Customers" and "Vendors" filter by type, and "Active leads" opens the pipeline.
- **Components:**
  - `Table` and `DataTable` take a controlled `page` / `onPageChange`, so a refresh keeps your page. `DataTable` takes `toolbarStart` in place of its search box.
  - The unused `usePagination` hook is removed.
  - `.next-e2e-dev` is ignored by ESLint.
- **Tests:**
  - New: `us-crm-22` and `us-crm-23` (web unit); `us-ops-36`, `us-crm-21`, `us-crm-22` and `us-crm-23` E2E.
  - Toggle, ToggleGroup, Table and DataTable a11y tests cover the tooltip, the controlled page and `toolbarStart`.
  - Steadier under a busy machine: the new specs share the seeded org (no extra demo seeds, which flood the email queue); `dashboard.spec` waits for the layout to save before reloading; `us-onb-22` wheels until Continue shows; `us-inv-23` gives the reminder email 15 s; `us-inv-18` accepts whichever invoice is most overdue in the shared org.
  - QA script section 202.
- **Design:** canvas "05 Contacts polish"; five artboards.

### 2026-10-10 — Dashboard polish: placeholders, figures that fit, no gaps and a side column (US-DASH-27 to US-DASH-29, D-209)

- **While it loads (US-DASH-27):**
  - Until the layout, rights and modules are known, the page shows its shape in grey and says "Loading dashboard" once (`DashboardSkeleton`, the body is `aria-busy`).
  - Then each card shows its title and a grey outline of its own content instead of a spinner (`WidgetSkeleton`); the KPI row keeps four grey cards (`KpiSkeleton`). No shimmer with Reduce Motion.
  - The organisation's currency is read once for the page (`DashboardCurrencyProvider`) and cards wait for it, so "$" never shows before "₹". The KPI row and the money chart wait for the Accrual/Cash choice, so they load once.
- **Figures that fit (US-DASH-28):**
  - KPI cards get an icon chip and a soft tint from the category palette (Cash blue, Profit teal, Receivables purple; Overdue keeps the status red), with the figure on its own line and the 8-week line across the card under it. A long figure steps down a size (container queries).
  - Payables and Tax this period stack their three figures when they don't fit side by side (`FigureTrio`).
  - The Receivables aging ring shows the total in short form ("₹44.9L") with the full total under the title: new `formatMoneyCompact` in `@spine/shared`. `DonutChart` puts its legend under the ring below 20rem and lets an amount wrap rather than be cut off (doc page and a11y test updated).
  - Amounts in Job profitability, Retention held, Top expenses and Ready to bill stay on one line; names beside them are cut short with the full name on hover.
- **No gaps, and a side column (US-DASH-29):**
  - Cards keep their own height and drop into the shortest column (a dense grid with measured row spans). Income and expenses, the one wide card, sits first after the KPI row; Jobs in progress is now one column with two-line rows.
  - From 1440 px (`wide-plus`, new in `tokens.json` and the Tailwind preset), "Needs your attention", "Today" and Recent activity sit in a column on the right that stays in view while it fits the window. Below that attention and Today stay at the top (the same height from 1280 px), with 3 card columns from 1280 px and 2 from 1024 px.
  - Edit layout keeps Recent activity in the grid so it can still be hidden; keyboard order is unchanged (tasks, KPI row, cards).
- **Tests:**
  - New: `us-dash-27` (web unit), `us-dash-28` (shared) and `us-dash-27` / `us-dash-28` / `us-dash-29` E2E.
  - `us-dash-20` reads Jobs in progress as a list; `us-dash-16` and `dashboard.spec` find Today by its heading.
  - QA script section 201.
- **Design:** canvas "04 Dashboard polish"; five `Dashboard*` artboards.

### 2026-10-10 — Onboarding in four stages and a finish screen (US-ONB-22, US-ONB-23, D-208)

- **Start screen:** `/onboarding` opens in the signed-out frame: "Welcome to Spine, <first name>", the four stages, the same three buttons, and Spine's dashboard, an invoice and the phone app drawn small with the people (`OnboardingStartIllustration`).
- **Four stages (US-ONB-22):**
  - The eight steps keep their forms and labels, grouped as Business · Money · Documents · Books & team (`ONBOARDING_STAGES`).
  - A rail (a bar below 1024 px) ticks steps off and counts down "About <n> min left"; every step has "Stage n of 4 · …", **Back** and a tip saying where to change it later (`OnboardingShell`).
- **Live preview (from 1280 px):**
  - The start of the first invoice, from what's typed before it's saved: name, logo and address, money in the chosen currency and locale, the tax line, and the first invoice number. The last stage shows "Your books", "Your team" and "Set so far" (`OnboardingPreview`).
  - Business, Currency, Tax country, Default tax rate and Numbering steps gain an optional `onChange`.
  - New `formatDocumentNumber` in `@spine/shared`, now also used by the numbering service, so the preview's number and the real one can't differ.
- **Finish screen (US-ONB-23):** Finish now ends on "You're set up": a summary with Change links (Settings panes over the screen), three next actions and "See what's left to set up — <n> left". Skipping and the demo business still go straight to the dashboard.
- **Finished means finished:** `/onboarding` now sends a finished or skipped onboarding to the dashboard (US-ONB-01), so the finish screen doesn't come back on refresh.
- **Reuse:** `AuthFrame` takes a custom `panel` and a `wide` column; `Figure`, `Caption` and `IllustrationPanel` are exported from the auth illustration.
- **Tests:**
  - New: `us-onb-22` (web unit and shared) and `us-onb-22` / `us-onb-23` E2E.
  - `us-ops-21` finds the step by its heading, since the rail now names every step.
  - QA script section 200.
- **Design:** canvas "03 Onboarding"; ten `Onb*` artboards.
- **Fixes after review:**
  - The wizard and every signed-out page now scroll with the wheel or trackpad in a short window. `globals.css` stops the body scrolling (the app shell scrolls its own content), so `OnboardingShell` and `AuthFrame` are their own scroll areas; the rail, the preview and the illustration stay in place.
  - In the preview, a long business name wraps instead of pushing the invoice number out of the card.
  - `us-onb-22` and `us-auth-14` now scroll with the mouse wheel, as a person would.

### 2026-10-10 — People in the signed-out illustration (US-AUTH-13, US-AUTH-14, D-207)

- **The illustration is now people:** welcome shows a team of three (a tradesperson pointing at the quote, the owner and a customer; two men, one woman) and login shows one man with a coffee. They're Open Peeps (CC0, Pablo Stanley), shipped as one sprite, `apps/web/public/illustrations/people.svg`, and drawn with `<use>`. The Spine mark and its orbits leave the panel.
- **No names or amounts on the cards:** each card is an icon, placeholder lines and its status (quote Sent → Accepted, invoice Due → Paid, payment Paid; login's today list and an Accepted quote).
- **Colour from tokens:** new `illustration` tokens (`--color-illustration-ink`, `-skin`, `-outfit-mint`, `-outfit-sky`, `-outfit-periwinkle`) in `tokens.json` and the preset, the same in light and dark like the brand colours; listed on the design-system colour page. The ground and halos use the logo's layer colours.
- **Holds together at any width:** the scene sits on a centred stage up to 700 px wide and sizes itself with container units.
- **Design:** canvas "02 Welcome, login and sign-up" redrawn with the people (chosen on "02b Signed-out illustration - people": Open Peeps, soft colours); all nine `Auth*` artboards re-exported.
- **Tests:** `us-auth-13` checks the team comes from the sprite, the cards carry no names or amounts, and the sprite parses (an XML comment containing two hyphens had blanked it).

### 2026-10-10 — Welcome, login and passwords (US-AUTH-13 to US-AUTH-15, D-207)

- **One frame for every signed-out page (`AuthFrame`):** the logo and theme toggle on top, the form centred, and beside it, from 1024 px up, an illustration of made-up work drawn with Spine's own card styles around the Spine mark. It's still under Reduce Motion and skipped by screen readers. It's used by welcome, login, sign-up, invite-only, forgot password, "Check your email", reset password and invitations; the old card shell is gone.
- **Welcome on a new install (US-AUTH-13):**
  - `/` and `/login` open `/welcome` while the install has no account, using `newInstall`, a new field on `GET /public/signup-status`; tests' forced open sign-up never sets it.
  - The page has "Run your business on Spine", the three next steps and **Create your account**, with the quote → job → invoice → paid story.
- **Login (US-AUTH-14):**
  - Email is focused, with show/hide on the password and a Caps Lock warning.
  - A wrong password reads "That email and password don't match. Check both, or reset your password." (the same as the phone), and too many tries says to wait a minute. While it works, the button reads "Logging in…".
  - The invitation sign-up says "Join <Business> on Spine" and the role.
- **Passwords (US-AUTH-15):**
  - `passwordStrength` in `@spine/shared` (a built-in checker, no new dependency) drives the new `PasswordInput` and `PasswordStrengthMeter` in `@spine/ui`, which have doc pages and accessibility tests.
  - Sign-up gains Confirm password; reset password and Profile › Password use the same fields and rules, and Profile now asks to confirm the new password.
  - The API refuses common passwords at sign-up, reset and change (400 `PASSWORD_TOO_COMMON`); the 8-character minimum is unchanged.
- **Tests:**
  - `apps/web` gains Vitest, with `us-auth-13` covering the middleware routing and the page.
  - New: `us-auth-15` (shared and API integration) and `us-auth-14` / `us-auth-15` E2E.
  - E2E password fields are now found with anchored labels (`/^Password/`), since "Show password" and "Confirm password" also match.
  - QA script section 199.

### 2026-10-09 — The Spine logo across the web app (US-SET-26, D-206, closes Q-25)

- **`SpineLogo` in `@spine/ui`:**
  - Draws the mark, horizontal or vertical lockup as inline SVG from the same artwork as the phone app, with a test keeping the two copies identical.
  - Read as "Spine" unless decorative, and clamped to the brand minimums.
  - The wordmark uses the new `--color-brand-wordmark` token (Spine blue, light text in dark), outside the theme rules, so a business's theme never recolours it. The brand teal, sky, deep and layer gradients are now web tokens too.
- **Where it shows:**
  - The sidebar (horizontal logo; collapsed, the mark alone, which is also the expand button).
  - Login, sign-up, onboarding, accept invite and the password pages.
  - The public quote page's "Sent with Spine" footer.
  - Dark mode now comes from CSS instead of a swap after load, and the old logo PNGs are gone.
- **Icons:**
  - An SVG favicon and `favicon.ico` (16/32/48) with a solid white mark that reads at tab size.
  - A 180 px Apple touch icon, and a web manifest with 192, 512 and maskable icons (`display: browser`, no install prompt).
  - The browser bar takes the page colour in light and dark.
  - Push notifications use the square app icon and a one-colour badge.
- **`SidebarHeader` (`@spine/ui`):** collapsed with a logo, the logo is the expand button, showing the expand icon on hover or focus.
- **Design system:** a SpineLogo doc page, Brand swatches on Color, and the new logo in the Sidebar, AppShell and Templates demos.
- **Tests:** `us-set-26` (unit, in `@spine/ui`) and `us-set-26.spec.ts` (E2E: the signed-out icons and manifest, the sidebar expanded and collapsed). QA script section 198.

### 2026-10-09 — CI confirms instead of discovering: `pnpm verify` and a faster, cheaper pipeline (D-205)

- **`pnpm verify`** runs what CI runs, locally and in CI's order: lint → typecheck → unit → integration → E2E.
  - It creates and migrates `spine_test` and `spine_e2e` when they're missing.
  - It stops at the first failure and says `pnpm verify --from <step>`; `--skip e2e` leaves a step out.
  - Run it before opening a PR or marking it ready.
- **CI (`.github/workflows/ci.yml`):**
  - "Lint, typecheck and unit" runs first, with no services (about 5 min). Integration and E2E then run side by side instead of one after the other.
  - Each suite runs only when the code it covers changed: a mobile-only change skips both.
  - The first of integration and E2E to fail cancels the other. Integration stops at five failing tests (`--bail=5`); E2E after five failing specs (`maxFailures`) and now keeps an HTML report for the failure artifact.
  - The required checks keep their names ("Unit and integration" is now a gate over the static and integration jobs), so `main`'s branch protection is unchanged.
- Expected: a full run goes from about 45 minutes of wall time to about 25, and a failure stops within minutes instead of running to the end.

### 2026-10-09 — Phase 5 Group 6: the Spine brand on the phone (US-MOB-01, US-MOB-24 to US-MOB-27)

- **App icon (US-MOB-24):**
  - iOS: the full-bleed gradient icon, plus dark and tinted versions (`ios.icon`).
  - Android: an adaptive icon (the gradient background, the mark at 62% inside the safe circle) and a themed-icon silhouette.
  - A 512 px Play Store icon.
  - The PNGs are rendered from SVGs committed in `apps/mobile/assets/brand/`. Expo's placeholder icon, splash and favicon are gone.
- **Splash (US-MOB-25):** `expo-splash-screen` shows the 96 dp mark on `#F7F6F3` / `#121110`. It's held until the session and the Welcome flag are known and the first screen has laid out, and never waits on the network.
- **Welcome (US-MOB-26):**
  - Shown once per install, before Sign in. The mark rises out of the splash and its layers breathe apart. Today's job, saved photos, a fuel receipt, a signed quote (its signature drawing itself) and a completed milestone slide out from behind it. Then "Today’s jobs, in your pocket" and "Get started".
  - Built with Reanimated 4 and `react-native-svg`: a gentle idle loop while on screen, and a still frame with Reduce Motion.
  - "Get started" replaces it with Sign in and sets the per-device flag; a phone already signed in skips it for good.
- **Brand (US-MOB-27):**
  - `tokens.json` gains `color.brand` and `color.gradients.brand`.
  - `@spine/ui-native` adds `brandColors` (no Tailwind class, so brand colours can't be used for text) and `SpineLogo`: mark, horizontal or vertical, drawn from the logo paths, dark-mode aware, read as "Spine", clamped to the brand minimum.
  - New "Spine logo" page and Brand swatches in `apps/design-system-native`.
- **Sign in (US-MOB-01):**
  - The vertical logo on a short version of Welcome's illustration, titled "Sign in". The illustration folds to the horizontal logo while the keyboard is up.
  - A wrong password reads "That email and password don't match. Check both, or reset your password."
- **Development builds:**
  - A launch from the dev-client link (`spine://expo-development-client/…`) now opens the first screen (`app/+native-intent.tsx`). Before, the router tried to open that link as a screen and raised a red error toast, which covered "Get started".
  - Reanimated's "Reduced motion setting is enabled" notice is silenced, since both screens handle Reduce Motion themselves.
- **End-to-end:** `us-mob-01-sign-in` passes on the iOS simulator (Welcome → Sign in → wrong password → Today → Profile → sign out, never back to Welcome). On the Android emulator, Maestro's driver hangs on `eraseText` in the Server field, with the previous Sign in screen as well; that flow is in the iOS e2e script only.
- **Tests:** `us-mob-24` to `us-mob-27` added; `us-mob-01` updated. Reanimated, Worklets and the splash are mocked in the mobile Jest setup. QA script section 197.
